Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the formal_languages space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 877 and the convergence rate is O(691/n^3).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to formal_languages structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 877/691 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Theorem 213.7 (Homomorphism Theorem)

Statement: Given a formal_languages algebra A over field F with homomorphism theorem conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 402. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 578/2.

Each subproblem has a solution by the homomorphism theorem fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
